Typical KR mobiles are 11 digits (010xxxxxxxx). Fall back to raw digits if unexpected.\n    if len(digits) == 11:\n        return f\"{digits[0:3]}-{digits[3:7]}-{digits[7:11]}\"\n    if len(digits) == 10:\n        return f\"{digits[0:3]}-{digits[3:6]}-{digits[6:10]}\"\n    return phone\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass ReplacementPlan:\n    placeholders: Dict[str, str]\n    birth_placeholder: str\n\n\ndef _build_replacement_plan(intake: Dict[str, str]) -> ReplacementPlan:\n    \"\"\"Map exact placeholders (including braces) to replacement strings.\"\"\"\n    birth = _parse_ymd(intake[\"생년월일\"])\n    visit = _parse_ymd(intake[\"방문일\"])\n    age = _korean_full_year_age(birth, visit)\n\n    # Birth date replacement must include age note immediately after the date.\n    birth_value_with_age = f\"{intake['생년월일']} ({age}세)\"\n\n    placeholders = {\n        \"{{환자명}}\": intake[\"환자명\"],\n        \"{{생년월일}}\": birth_value_with_age,\n        \"{{방문일}}\": intake[\"방문일\"],\n        \"{{진료팀}}\": intake[\"진료팀\"],\n        \"{{회신전화}}\": _normalize_phone_3_4_4(intake[\"회신전화\"]),\n        \"{{증상요약}}\": intake[\"증상요약\"],\n        \"{{보험상태}}\": intake[\"보험상태\"],\n    }\n\n    return ReplacementPlan(placeholders=placeholders, birth_placeholder=\"{{생년월일}}\")\n\n\ndef _is_simple_text_run(run_el: ET.Element) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"True if this run is a simple text run (<...:run><...:t>TEXT</...:t></...:run>).\"\"\"\n    # Only element children count here; replace its text.\n    first_t = next(iter(runs[0]))\n    if (first_t.text or \"\") != merged:\n        first_t.text = merged\n\n    # Remove the other run elements entirely.\n    for r in runs[1:]:\n        p_el.remove(r)\n\n    return True\n\n\ndef _replace_placeholders_in_paragraph(p_el: ET.Element, plan: ReplacementPlan) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Replace placeholders in all <...:t> text nodes in a paragraph.\n\n    Returns True if any text node changed.\n    \"\"\"\n    changed = False\n\n    for t_el in p_el.iter():\n        if _localname(t_el.tag) != \"t\":\n            continue\n        if not (t_el.text and \"{{\" in t_el.text):\n            continue\n\n        new_text = t_el.text\n        for ph, value in plan.placeholders.items():\n            if ph in new_text:\n                new_text = new_text.replace(ph, value)\n\n        if new_text != t_el.text:\n            t_el.text = new_text\n            changed = True\n\n    return changed\n\n\ndef _remove_layout_cache_children(p_el: ET.Element) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Remove known layout-cache elements from a modified paragraph.\n\n    In many HWPX files, <...:linesegarray> is a cache that should be regenerated.\n\n    Returns True if anything was removed.\n    \"\"\"\n    cache_localnames = {\n        \"linesegarray\",\n        \"lineseg\",\n        \"linesegArray\",\n        \"lineSegArray\",\n        \"lineSeg\",\n    }\n\n    removed = False\n    # These caches are typically direct children of <...:p>.\n    for child in list(p_el):\n        ln = _localname(child.tag)\n        if ln in cache_localnames or ln.lower() in cache_localnames:\n            p_el.remove(child)\n            removed = True\n\n    return removed\n\n\ndef _process_xml_file(path: Path, plan: ReplacementPlan) -> Tuple[bool, int]:\n    \"\"\"Process a single XML file. Returns (changed, modified_paragraph_count).\"\"\"\n    raw = path.read_bytes()\n\n    tree = ET.ElementTree(ET.fromstring(raw))\n    root = tree.getroot()\n\n    changed_any = False\n    modified_paras = 0\n\n    # Process each paragraph element.\n    for p_el in root.iter():\n        if _localname(p_el.tag) != \"p\":\n            continue\n\n        merged = _merge_adjacent_text_runs_in_paragraph(p_el)\n        replaced = _replace_placeholders_in_paragraph(p_el, plan)\n\n        if merged or replaced:\n            # Only clear caches when text/structure changed.\n            _remove_layout_cache_children(p_el)\n            changed_any = True\n            modified_paras += 1\n\n    if changed_any:\n        xml_bytes = ET.tostring(root, encoding=\"utf-8\", xml_declaration=True)\n        path.write_bytes(xml_bytes)\n\n    return changed_any, modified_paras\n\n\ndef _assert_no_placeholders_remain(extracted_root: Path) -> None:\n    leftovers: List[Tuple[str, str]] = []\n    for p in extracted_root.rglob(\"*\"):\n        if not p.is_file():\n            continue\n        # Only scan text-like files;
